#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;

// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    return j * n + i;
}

void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    }
    
    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    }
}

void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = j;
            path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = i;
        }
        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
    }
}

void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                   const size_t numNodes) {
    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
    // For each intermediate node k
    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
        // For each source node i
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            // For each destination node j
            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
                
                if (newDist < distIJ) {
                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                // Check for overflow before addition
                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", 
                               i, j, k);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numNodes = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", numNodes);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);
    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing graph...\n");
    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);
    
    // Run Floyd-Warshall
    printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate operations per second
    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity
    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)
    if (printResults) {
        print_results_int(dist, "DistanceMatrix");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

+struct Block {
+    size_t begin;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+Block blockFor(const size_t n, const int coordinate, const int parts) {
+    const size_t width = n / parts;
+    const size_t remainder = n % parts;
+    const size_t c = static_cast<size_t>(coordinate);
+    return {c * width + std::min(c, remainder), width + (c < remainder)};
+}
+
+int ownerOf(const size_t k, const size_t n, const int parts) {
+    const size_t width = n / parts;
+    const size_t remainder = n % parts;
+    const size_t boundary = (width + 1) * remainder;
+    // When width is zero, every valid k is in the first branch.
+    return static_cast<int>(k < boundary ? k / (width + 1)
+                                        : remainder + (k - boundary) / width);

+struct ProcessGrid {
+    int dimensions[2] = {0, 0};
+    int row;
+    int column;
+    Block rows;
+    Block columns;
+    MPI_Comm rowComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    MPI_Comm columnComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+
+    ProcessGrid(const size_t n, const int rank, const int processes) {
+        MPI_Dims_create(processes, 2, dimensions);
+        row = rank / dimensions[1];
+        column = rank % dimensions[1];
+        rows = blockFor(n, row, dimensions[0]);
+        columns = blockFor(n, column, dimensions[1]);
+        // Communicator ranks coincide with the corresponding grid coordinate.
+        MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, row, column, &rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, column, row, &columnComm);
+    }
+
+    ~ProcessGrid() {
+        MPI_Comm_free(&rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&columnComm);

+                    static_cast<int>(stride), MPI_UNSIGNED, &type);
+    MPI_Type_commit(&type);
+    return type;

+// Separate pivot buffers and non-aliasing rows allow SIMD masked updates.
+inline void updateRow(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                      unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                      const unsigned int* __restrict__ pivotRow,
+                      const size_t columns, const unsigned int distIK,
+                      const unsigned int k) {
+    if (distIK == 0) {
+        return; // The zero-diagonal pivot row cannot improve itself.

+    for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; ++j) {
+        const unsigned int candidate = distIK + pivotRow[j];
+        if (candidate < dist[j]) {

-void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {

+void broadcastPivot(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t n,
+                    const size_t k, const ProcessGrid& grid,
+                    std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotRow,
+                    std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotColumn,
+                    MPI_Request* requests) {
+    const int rowOwner = ownerOf(k, n, grid.dimensions[0]);
+    const int columnOwner = ownerOf(k, n, grid.dimensions[1]);
+    if (grid.row == rowOwner && grid.columns.count != 0) {
+        std::copy_n(dist.data() + (k - grid.rows.begin) * grid.columns.count,
+                    grid.columns.count, pivotRow.data());
+    }
+    if (grid.column == columnOwner) {
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.rows.count; ++i) {
+            pivotColumn[i] = dist[i * grid.columns.count + k - grid.columns.begin];
+        }
+    }
+    MPI_Ibcast(pivotRow.data(), static_cast<int>(grid.columns.count), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+               rowOwner, grid.columnComm, &requests[0]);
+    MPI_Ibcast(pivotColumn.data(), static_cast<int>(grid.rows.count), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+               columnOwner, grid.rowComm, &requests[1]);
+}
+
+void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t n,
+                   const ProcessGrid& grid) {
+    if (n == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotRows[2] = {
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(grid.columns.count),
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(grid.columns.count)};
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotColumns[2] = {
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(grid.rows.count),
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(grid.rows.count)};
+    MPI_Request requests[2][2];
+    broadcastPivot(dist, n, 0, grid, pivotRows[0], pivotColumns[0], requests[0]);
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
+        const size_t slot = k % 2;
+        MPI_Waitall(2, requests[slot], MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+        const unsigned int* pivotRow = pivotRows[slot].data();
+        const unsigned int* pivotColumn = pivotColumns[slot].data();
+
+        if (k + 1 < n) {
+            // Update the next pivot's row and column first, then communicate them
+            // while the rest of this iteration is computed. All k remain ordered.
+            const size_t next = k + 1;
+            if (next >= grid.rows.begin && next - grid.rows.begin < grid.rows.count
+                && grid.columns.count != 0) {
+                const size_t i = next - grid.rows.begin;
+                updateRow(dist.data() + i * grid.columns.count,
+                          path.data() + i * grid.columns.count, pivotRow,
+                          grid.columns.count, pivotColumn[i],
+                          static_cast<unsigned int>(k));
+            }
+            if (next >= grid.columns.begin &&
+                next - grid.columns.begin < grid.columns.count) {
+                const size_t j = next - grid.columns.begin;
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.rows.count; ++i) {
+                    const size_t offset = i * grid.columns.count + j;
+                    const unsigned int candidate = pivotColumn[i] + pivotRow[j];
+                    if (candidate < dist[offset]) {
+                        dist[offset] = candidate;
+                        path[offset] = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            const size_t nextSlot = 1 - slot;
+            broadcastPivot(dist, n, next, grid, pivotRows[nextSlot],
+                           pivotColumns[nextSlot], requests[nextSlot]);
+        }
+
+        if (grid.columns.count != 0) {
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.rows.count; ++i) {
+                // Repeating the early row/column updates is idempotent: pivot
+                // distances are unchanged with these nonnegative, bounded weights.
+                updateRow(dist.data() + i * grid.columns.count,
+                          path.data() + i * grid.columns.count, pivotRow,
+                          grid.columns.count, pivotColumn[i],
+                          static_cast<unsigned int>(k));
+            }
         }
-        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
     }
 }

+std::vector<unsigned int> gatherDistances(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                                         const size_t n, const ProcessGrid& grid,
+                                         const int rank, const MPI_Datatype localType) {
+    std::vector<unsigned int> result;
+    if (rank != 0) {
+        if (!dist.empty()) {
+            MPI_Send(dist.data(), 1, localType, 0, 1, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+        return result;
+    }
+
+    result.resize(n * n);
+    for (int r = 0; r < grid.dimensions[0]; ++r) {
+        const Block rows = blockFor(n, r, grid.dimensions[0]);
+        for (int c = 0; c < grid.dimensions[1]; ++c) {
+            const Block columns = blockFor(n, c, grid.dimensions[1]);
+            if (rows.count == 0 || columns.count == 0) {
+                continue;
+            }
+            const int source = r * grid.dimensions[1] + c;
+            unsigned int* target = result.data() + rows.begin * n + columns.begin;
+            if (source == 0) {
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < rows.count; ++i) {
+                    std::copy_n(dist.data() + i * columns.count, columns.count,
+                                target + i * n);
                 }
+            } else {
+                MPI_Datatype type = matrixBlockType(rows.count, columns.count, n);
+                MPI_Recv(target, 1, type, source, 1, MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                MPI_Type_free(&type);
             }
         }
     }
+    return result;
 }

+                                         const size_t n, const ProcessGrid& grid,
+                                         const int rank, const MPI_Datatype localType) {
+    std::vector<unsigned int> result;
+    if (rank != 0) {
+        if (!dist.empty()) {
+            MPI_Send(dist.data(), 1, localType, 0, 1, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+        return result;
+    }

+    result.resize(n * n);
+    for (int r = 0; r < grid.dimensions[0]; ++r) {
+        const Block rows = blockFor(n, r, grid.dimensions[0]);
+        for (int c = 0; c < grid.dimensions[1]; ++c) {
+            const Block columns = blockFor(n, c, grid.dimensions[1]);
+            if (rows.count == 0 || columns.count == 0) {
+                continue;

+            unsigned int* target = result.data() + rows.begin * n + columns.begin;

+                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                MPI_Type_free(&type);
             }
         }
     }

-void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes,

+struct Block {
+    size_t begin;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+Block blockFor(const size_t n, const int coordinate, const int parts) {
+    const size_t width = n / parts;
+    const size_t remainder = n % parts;
+    const size_t c = static_cast<size_t>(coordinate);
+    return {c * width + std::min(c, remainder), width + (c < remainder)};
+}
+
+int ownerOf(const size_t k, const size_t n, const int parts) {
+    const size_t width = n / parts;
+    const size_t remainder = n % parts;
+    const size_t boundary = (width + 1) * remainder;
+    // When width is zero, every valid k is in the first branch.
+    return static_cast<int>(k < boundary ? k / (width + 1)
+                                        : remainder + (k - boundary) / width);
+}
+
+struct ProcessGrid {
+    int dimensions[2] = {0, 0};
+    int row;
+    int column;
+    Block rows;
+    Block columns;
+    MPI_Comm rowComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    MPI_Comm columnComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+
+    ProcessGrid(const size_t n, const int rank, const int processes) {
+        MPI_Dims_create(processes, 2, dimensions);
+        row = rank / dimensions[1];
+        column = rank % dimensions[1];
+        rows = blockFor(n, row, dimensions[0]);
+        columns = blockFor(n, column, dimensions[1]);
+        // Communicator ranks coincide with the corresponding grid coordinate.
+        MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, row, column, &rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, column, row, &columnComm);
+    }
+
+    ~ProcessGrid() {
+        MPI_Comm_free(&rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&columnComm);
+    }
+};
+
+MPI_Datatype matrixBlockType(const size_t rows, const size_t columns,
+                            const size_t stride) {
+    MPI_Datatype type;
+    // Using one derived datatype also supports blocks exceeding INT_MAX elements.
+    MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(rows), static_cast<int>(columns),
+                    static_cast<int>(stride), MPI_UNSIGNED, &type);
+    MPI_Type_commit(&type);
+    return type;
+}
+

+// Separate pivot buffers and non-aliasing rows allow SIMD masked updates.
+inline void updateRow(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                      unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                      const unsigned int* __restrict__ pivotRow,
+                      const size_t columns, const unsigned int distIK,
+                      const unsigned int k) {
+    if (distIK == 0) {
+        return; // The zero-diagonal pivot row cannot improve itself.
+    }
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; ++j) {
+        const unsigned int candidate = distIK + pivotRow[j];
+        if (candidate < dist[j]) {
+            dist[j] = candidate;
+            path[j] = k;
+        }
     }

+void broadcastPivot(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t n,
+                    const size_t k, const ProcessGrid& grid,
+                    std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotRow,
+                    std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotColumn,
+                    MPI_Request* requests) {

+    const int columnOwner = ownerOf(k, n, grid.dimensions[1]);

-    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
-    // For each intermediate node k

-        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {

-                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
+void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t n,
+                   const ProcessGrid& grid) {
+    if (n == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotRows[2] = {
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(grid.columns.count),
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(grid.columns.count)};
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotColumns[2] = {
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(grid.rows.count),
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(grid.rows.count)};
+    MPI_Request requests[2][2];
+    broadcastPivot(dist, n, 0, grid, pivotRows[0], pivotColumns[0], requests[0]);
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
+        const size_t slot = k % 2;
+        MPI_Waitall(2, requests[slot], MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+        const unsigned int* pivotRow = pivotRows[slot].data();
+        const unsigned int* pivotColumn = pivotColumns[slot].data();
+
+        if (k + 1 < n) {
+            // Update the next pivot's row and column first, then communicate them
+            // while the rest of this iteration is computed. All k remain ordered.
+            const size_t next = k + 1;
+            if (next >= grid.rows.begin && next - grid.rows.begin < grid.rows.count
+                && grid.columns.count != 0) {
+                const size_t i = next - grid.rows.begin;
+                updateRow(dist.data() + i * grid.columns.count,
+                          path.data() + i * grid.columns.count, pivotRow,
+                          grid.columns.count, pivotColumn[i],
+                          static_cast<unsigned int>(k));
+            }
+            if (next >= grid.columns.begin &&
+                next - grid.columns.begin < grid.columns.count) {
+                const size_t j = next - grid.columns.begin;
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.rows.count; ++i) {
+                    const size_t offset = i * grid.columns.count + j;
+                    const unsigned int candidate = pivotColumn[i] + pivotRow[j];
+                    if (candidate < dist[offset]) {
+                        dist[offset] = candidate;
+                        path[offset] = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
+                    }
                 }
             }
+            const size_t nextSlot = 1 - slot;
+            broadcastPivot(dist, n, next, grid, pivotRows[nextSlot],
+                           pivotColumns[nextSlot], requests[nextSlot]);
+        }
+
+        if (grid.columns.count != 0) {
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.rows.count; ++i) {
+                // Repeating the early row/column updates is idempotent: pivot
+                // distances are unchanged with these nonnegative, bounded weights.
+                updateRow(dist.data() + i * grid.columns.count,
+                          path.data() + i * grid.columns.count, pivotRow,
+                          grid.columns.count, pivotColumn[i],
+                          static_cast<unsigned int>(k));
+            }
         }
     }
 }
 
+std::vector<unsigned int> gatherDistances(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                                         const size_t n, const ProcessGrid& grid,
+                                         const int rank, const MPI_Datatype localType) {
+    std::vector<unsigned int> result;
+    if (rank != 0) {
+        if (!dist.empty()) {
+            MPI_Send(dist.data(), 1, localType, 0, 1, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+        return result;
+    }
+
+    result.resize(n * n);
+    for (int r = 0; r < grid.dimensions[0]; ++r) {
+        const Block rows = blockFor(n, r, grid.dimensions[0]);
+        for (int c = 0; c < grid.dimensions[1]; ++c) {
+            const Block columns = blockFor(n, c, grid.dimensions[1]);
+            if (rows.count == 0 || columns.count == 0) {
+                continue;
+            }
+            const int source = r * grid.dimensions[1] + c;
+            unsigned int* target = result.data() + rows.begin * n + columns.begin;
+            if (source == 0) {
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < rows.count; ++i) {
+                    std::copy_n(dist.data() + i * columns.count, columns.count,
+                                target + i * n);
+                }
+            } else {
+                MPI_Datatype type = matrixBlockType(rows.count, columns.count, n);
+                MPI_Recv(target, 1, type, source, 1, MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                MPI_Type_free(&type);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    return result;
+}

-void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
-                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
+struct Block {
+    size_t begin;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+Block blockFor(const size_t n, const int coordinate, const int parts) {
+    const size_t width = n / parts;
+    const size_t remainder = n % parts;
+    const size_t c = static_cast<size_t>(coordinate);
+    return {c * width + std::min(c, remainder), width + (c < remainder)};
+}
+
+int ownerOf(const size_t k, const size_t n, const int parts) {
+    const size_t width = n / parts;
+    const size_t remainder = n % parts;
+    const size_t boundary = (width + 1) * remainder;
+    // When width is zero, every valid k is in the first branch.
+    return static_cast<int>(k < boundary ? k / (width + 1)
+                                        : remainder + (k - boundary) / width);
+}
+
+struct ProcessGrid {
+    int dimensions[2] = {0, 0};
+    int row;
+    int column;
+    Block rows;
+    Block columns;
+    MPI_Comm rowComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    MPI_Comm columnComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+
+    ProcessGrid(const size_t n, const int rank, const int processes) {
+        MPI_Dims_create(processes, 2, dimensions);
+        row = rank / dimensions[1];
+        column = rank % dimensions[1];
+        rows = blockFor(n, row, dimensions[0]);
+        columns = blockFor(n, column, dimensions[1]);
+        // Communicator ranks coincide with the corresponding grid coordinate.
+        MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, row, column, &rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, column, row, &columnComm);
+    }
+
+    ~ProcessGrid() {
+        MPI_Comm_free(&rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&columnComm);
+    }
+};
+
+MPI_Datatype matrixBlockType(const size_t rows, const size_t columns,
+                            const size_t stride) {
+    MPI_Datatype type;
+    // Using one derived datatype also supports blocks exceeding INT_MAX elements.
+    MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(rows), static_cast<int>(columns),
+                    static_cast<int>(stride), MPI_UNSIGNED, &type);
+    MPI_Type_commit(&type);
+    return type;
+}
+
+void initializeMatrices(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                        std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t n,
+                        const ProcessGrid& grid, const int rank,
+                        const MPI_Datatype localType) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.rows.count && grid.columns.count != 0; ++i) {
+        // The original path matrix starts with the source row index.
+        std::fill_n(path.data() + i * grid.columns.count, grid.columns.count,
+                    static_cast<unsigned int>(grid.rows.begin + i));
+    }
+
+    if (rank != 0) {
+        if (!dist.empty()) {
+            MPI_Recv(dist.data(), 1, localType, 0, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                     MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+        }
+        return;
+    }
+

+    const double range = static_cast<double>(MAX_DISTANCE);
+    std::vector<unsigned int> strip;

+    // No process retains a replicated global matrix during the computation.
+    for (int r = 0; r < grid.dimensions[0]; ++r) {
+        const Block rows = blockFor(n, r, grid.dimensions[0]);
+        if (rows.count == 0) {
+            continue;
+        }
+        strip.resize(rows.count * n);
+        for (auto& value : strip) {

+                                                  static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX));

+                    std::copy_n(strip.data() + i * n + columns.begin,
+                                columns.count, dist.data() + i * columns.count);
+                }

+
+// Separate pivot buffers and non-aliasing rows allow SIMD masked updates.
+inline void updateRow(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                      unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                      const unsigned int* __restrict__ pivotRow,
+                      const size_t columns, const unsigned int distIK,
+                      const unsigned int k) {
+    if (distIK == 0) {
+        return; // The zero-diagonal pivot row cannot improve itself.
+    }
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; ++j) {
+        const unsigned int candidate = distIK + pivotRow[j];
+        if (candidate < dist[j]) {
+            dist[j] = candidate;
+            path[j] = k;
+        }
     }
 }

+void broadcastPivot(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t n,
+                    const size_t k, const ProcessGrid& grid,
+                    std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotRow,
+                    std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotColumn,
+                    MPI_Request* requests) {
+    const int rowOwner = ownerOf(k, n, grid.dimensions[0]);
+    const int columnOwner = ownerOf(k, n, grid.dimensions[1]);
+    if (grid.row == rowOwner && grid.columns.count != 0) {
+        std::copy_n(dist.data() + (k - grid.rows.begin) * grid.columns.count,
+                    grid.columns.count, pivotRow.data());
+    }
+    if (grid.column == columnOwner) {
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.rows.count; ++i) {
+            pivotColumn[i] = dist[i * grid.columns.count + k - grid.columns.begin];
         }
-        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
     }
+    MPI_Ibcast(pivotRow.data(), static_cast<int>(grid.columns.count), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+               rowOwner, grid.columnComm, &requests[0]);
+    MPI_Ibcast(pivotColumn.data(), static_cast<int>(grid.rows.count), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+               columnOwner, grid.rowComm, &requests[1]);
 }

-    // For each intermediate node k

-                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
+void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t n,
+                   const ProcessGrid& grid) {
+    if (n == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotRows[2] = {
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(grid.columns.count),
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(grid.columns.count)};
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotColumns[2] = {
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(grid.rows.count),
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(grid.rows.count)};
+    MPI_Request requests[2][2];
+    broadcastPivot(dist, n, 0, grid, pivotRows[0], pivotColumns[0], requests[0]);
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
+        const size_t slot = k % 2;
+        MPI_Waitall(2, requests[slot], MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+        const unsigned int* pivotRow = pivotRows[slot].data();
+        const unsigned int* pivotColumn = pivotColumns[slot].data();
+
+        if (k + 1 < n) {
+            // Update the next pivot's row and column first, then communicate them
+            // while the rest of this iteration is computed. All k remain ordered.
+            const size_t next = k + 1;
+            if (next >= grid.rows.begin && next - grid.rows.begin < grid.rows.count
+                && grid.columns.count != 0) {
+                const size_t i = next - grid.rows.begin;
+                updateRow(dist.data() + i * grid.columns.count,
+                          path.data() + i * grid.columns.count, pivotRow,
+                          grid.columns.count, pivotColumn[i],
+                          static_cast<unsigned int>(k));
+            }
+            if (next >= grid.columns.begin &&
+                next - grid.columns.begin < grid.columns.count) {
+                const size_t j = next - grid.columns.begin;
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.rows.count; ++i) {
+                    const size_t offset = i * grid.columns.count + j;
+                    const unsigned int candidate = pivotColumn[i] + pivotRow[j];
+                    if (candidate < dist[offset]) {
+                        dist[offset] = candidate;
+                        path[offset] = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
+                    }
                 }
             }
+            const size_t nextSlot = 1 - slot;
+            broadcastPivot(dist, n, next, grid, pivotRows[nextSlot],
+                           pivotColumns[nextSlot], requests[nextSlot]);
+        }
+
+        if (grid.columns.count != 0) {
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.rows.count; ++i) {
+                // Repeating the early row/column updates is idempotent: pivot
+                // distances are unchanged with these nonnegative, bounded weights.
+                updateRow(dist.data() + i * grid.columns.count,
+                          path.data() + i * grid.columns.count, pivotRow,
+                          grid.columns.count, pivotColumn[i],
+                          static_cast<unsigned int>(k));
+            }
         }
     }
 }
 
+std::vector<unsigned int> gatherDistances(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                                         const size_t n, const ProcessGrid& grid,
+                                         const int rank, const MPI_Datatype localType) {
+    std::vector<unsigned int> result;
+    if (rank != 0) {
+        if (!dist.empty()) {
+            MPI_Send(dist.data(), 1, localType, 0, 1, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+        return result;
+    }
+
+    result.resize(n * n);
+    for (int r = 0; r < grid.dimensions[0]; ++r) {
+        const Block rows = blockFor(n, r, grid.dimensions[0]);
+        for (int c = 0; c < grid.dimensions[1]; ++c) {
+            const Block columns = blockFor(n, c, grid.dimensions[1]);
+            if (rows.count == 0 || columns.count == 0) {
+                continue;
+            }
+            const int source = r * grid.dimensions[1] + c;
+            unsigned int* target = result.data() + rows.begin * n + columns.begin;
+            if (source == 0) {
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < rows.count; ++i) {
+                    std::copy_n(dist.data() + i * columns.count, columns.count,
+                                target + i * n);
+                }
+            } else {
+                MPI_Datatype type = matrixBlockType(rows.count, columns.count, n);
+                MPI_Recv(target, 1, type, source, 1, MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                MPI_Type_free(&type);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    return result;
+}
+

#include <algorithm>
#include <cerrno>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <exception>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include <mpi.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;

// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    return j * n + i;
}

struct Block {
    size_t begin;
    size_t count;
};

Block blockFor(const size_t n, const int coordinate, const int parts) {
    const size_t width = n / parts;
    const size_t remainder = n % parts;
    const size_t c = static_cast<size_t>(coordinate);
    return {c * width + std::min(c, remainder), width + (c < remainder)};
}

int ownerOf(const size_t k, const size_t n, const int parts) {
    const size_t width = n / parts;
    const size_t remainder = n % parts;
    const size_t boundary = (width + 1) * remainder;
    // When width is zero, every valid k is in the first branch.
    return static_cast<int>(k < boundary ? k / (width + 1)
                                        : remainder + (k - boundary) / width);
}

struct ProcessGrid {
    int dimensions[2] = {0, 0};
    int row;
    int column;
    Block rows;
    Block columns;
    MPI_Comm rowComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
    MPI_Comm columnComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;

    ProcessGrid(const size_t n, const int rank, const int processes) {
        MPI_Dims_create(processes, 2, dimensions);
        row = rank / dimensions[1];
        column = rank % dimensions[1];
        rows = blockFor(n, row, dimensions[0]);
        columns = blockFor(n, column, dimensions[1]);
        // Communicator ranks coincide with the corresponding grid coordinate.
        MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, row, column, &rowComm);
        MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, column, row, &columnComm);
    }

    ~ProcessGrid() {
        MPI_Comm_free(&rowComm);
        MPI_Comm_free(&columnComm);
    }
};

MPI_Datatype matrixBlockType(const size_t rows, const size_t columns,
                            const size_t stride) {
    MPI_Datatype type;
    // Using one derived datatype also supports blocks exceeding INT_MAX elements.
    MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(rows), static_cast<int>(columns),
                    static_cast<int>(stride), MPI_UNSIGNED, &type);
    MPI_Type_commit(&type);
    return type;
}

void initializeMatrices(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
                        std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t n,
                        const ProcessGrid& grid, const int rank,
                        const MPI_Datatype localType) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.rows.count && grid.columns.count != 0; ++i) {
        // The original path matrix starts with the source row index.
        std::fill_n(path.data() + i * grid.columns.count, grid.columns.count,
                    static_cast<unsigned int>(grid.rows.begin + i));
    }

    if (rank != 0) {
        if (!dist.empty()) {
            MPI_Recv(dist.data(), 1, localType, 0, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD,
                     MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
        }
        return;
    }

    unsigned int seed = 42;
    const double range = static_cast<double>(MAX_DISTANCE);
    std::vector<unsigned int> strip;
    // Generate the original rand_r stream in order, staging only one row strip.
    // No process retains a replicated global matrix during the computation.
    for (int r = 0; r < grid.dimensions[0]; ++r) {
        const Block rows = blockFor(n, r, grid.dimensions[0]);
        if (rows.count == 0) {
            continue;
        }
        strip.resize(rows.count * n);
        for (auto& value : strip) {
            value = 1 + static_cast<unsigned int>(range * rand_r(&seed) /
                                                  static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX));
        }
        for (size_t i = 0; i < rows.count; ++i) {
            strip[i * n + rows.begin + i] = 0;
        }
        for (int c = 0; c < grid.dimensions[1]; ++c) {
            const Block columns = blockFor(n, c, grid.dimensions[1]);
            if (columns.count == 0) {
                continue;
            }
            const int destination = r * grid.dimensions[1] + c;
            if (destination == 0) {
                for (size_t i = 0; i < rows.count; ++i) {
                    std::copy_n(strip.data() + i * n + columns.begin,
                                columns.count, dist.data() + i * columns.count);
                }
            } else {
                MPI_Datatype type = matrixBlockType(rows.count, columns.count, n);
                MPI_Send(strip.data() + columns.begin, 1, type, destination, 0,
                         MPI_COMM_WORLD);
                MPI_Type_free(&type);
            }
        }
    }
}

// Separate pivot buffers and non-aliasing rows allow SIMD masked updates.
inline void updateRow(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
                      unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
                      const unsigned int* __restrict__ pivotRow,
                      const size_t columns, const unsigned int distIK,
                      const unsigned int k) {
    if (distIK == 0) {
        return; // The zero-diagonal pivot row cannot improve itself.
    }
    for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; ++j) {
        const unsigned int candidate = distIK + pivotRow[j];
        if (candidate < dist[j]) {
            dist[j] = candidate;
            path[j] = k;
        }
    }
}

void broadcastPivot(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t n,
                    const size_t k, const ProcessGrid& grid,
                    std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotRow,
                    std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotColumn,
                    MPI_Request* requests) {
    const int rowOwner = ownerOf(k, n, grid.dimensions[0]);
    const int columnOwner = ownerOf(k, n, grid.dimensions[1]);
    if (grid.row == rowOwner && grid.columns.count != 0) {
        std::copy_n(dist.data() + (k - grid.rows.begin) * grid.columns.count,
                    grid.columns.count, pivotRow.data());
    }
    if (grid.column == columnOwner) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.rows.count; ++i) {
            pivotColumn[i] = dist[i * grid.columns.count + k - grid.columns.begin];
        }
    }
    MPI_Ibcast(pivotRow.data(), static_cast<int>(grid.columns.count), MPI_UNSIGNED,
               rowOwner, grid.columnComm, &requests[0]);
    MPI_Ibcast(pivotColumn.data(), static_cast<int>(grid.rows.count), MPI_UNSIGNED,
               columnOwner, grid.rowComm, &requests[1]);
}

void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t n,
                   const ProcessGrid& grid) {
    if (n == 0) {
        return;
    }
    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotRows[2] = {
        std::vector<unsigned int>(grid.columns.count),
        std::vector<unsigned int>(grid.columns.count)};
    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotColumns[2] = {
        std::vector<unsigned int>(grid.rows.count),
        std::vector<unsigned int>(grid.rows.count)};
    MPI_Request requests[2][2];
    broadcastPivot(dist, n, 0, grid, pivotRows[0], pivotColumns[0], requests[0]);

    for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
        const size_t slot = k % 2;
        MPI_Waitall(2, requests[slot], MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
        const unsigned int* pivotRow = pivotRows[slot].data();
        const unsigned int* pivotColumn = pivotColumns[slot].data();

        if (k + 1 < n) {
            // Update the next pivot's row and column first, then communicate them
            // while the rest of this iteration is computed. All k remain ordered.
            const size_t next = k + 1;
            if (next >= grid.rows.begin && next - grid.rows.begin < grid.rows.count
                && grid.columns.count != 0) {
                const size_t i = next - grid.rows.begin;
                updateRow(dist.data() + i * grid.columns.count,
                          path.data() + i * grid.columns.count, pivotRow,
                          grid.columns.count, pivotColumn[i],
                          static_cast<unsigned int>(k));
            }
            if (next >= grid.columns.begin &&
                next - grid.columns.begin < grid.columns.count) {
                const size_t j = next - grid.columns.begin;
                for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.rows.count; ++i) {
                    const size_t offset = i * grid.columns.count + j;
                    const unsigned int candidate = pivotColumn[i] + pivotRow[j];
                    if (candidate < dist[offset]) {
                        dist[offset] = candidate;
                        path[offset] = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
                    }
                }
            }
            const size_t nextSlot = 1 - slot;
            broadcastPivot(dist, n, next, grid, pivotRows[nextSlot],
                           pivotColumns[nextSlot], requests[nextSlot]);
        }

        if (grid.columns.count != 0) {
            for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.rows.count; ++i) {
                // Repeating the early row/column updates is idempotent: pivot
                // distances are unchanged with these nonnegative, bounded weights.
                updateRow(dist.data() + i * grid.columns.count,
                          path.data() + i * grid.columns.count, pivotRow,
                          grid.columns.count, pivotColumn[i],
                          static_cast<unsigned int>(k));
            }
        }
    }
}

std::vector<unsigned int> gatherDistances(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
                                         const size_t n, const ProcessGrid& grid,
                                         const int rank, const MPI_Datatype localType) {
    std::vector<unsigned int> result;
    if (rank != 0) {
        if (!dist.empty()) {
            MPI_Send(dist.data(), 1, localType, 0, 1, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
        }
        return result;
    }

    result.resize(n * n);
    for (int r = 0; r < grid.dimensions[0]; ++r) {
        const Block rows = blockFor(n, r, grid.dimensions[0]);
        for (int c = 0; c < grid.dimensions[1]; ++c) {
            const Block columns = blockFor(n, c, grid.dimensions[1]);
            if (rows.count == 0 || columns.count == 0) {
                continue;
            }
            const int source = r * grid.dimensions[1] + c;
            unsigned int* target = result.data() + rows.begin * n + columns.begin;
            if (source == 0) {
                for (size_t i = 0; i < rows.count; ++i) {
                    std::copy_n(dist.data() + i * columns.count, columns.count,
                                target + i * n);
                }
            } else {
                MPI_Datatype type = matrixBlockType(rows.count, columns.count, n);
                MPI_Recv(target, 1, type, source, 1, MPI_COMM_WORLD,
                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
                MPI_Type_free(&type);
            }
        }
    }
    return result;
}

    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {

+            if (next >= grid.rows.begin && next - grid.rows.begin < grid.rows.count
+                && grid.columns.count != 0) {
+                const size_t i = next - grid.rows.begin;
+                updateRow(dist.data() + i * grid.columns.count,
+                          path.data() + i * grid.columns.count, pivotRow,
+                          grid.columns.count, pivotColumn[i],
+                          static_cast<unsigned int>(k));
+            }
+            if (next >= grid.columns.begin &&
+                next - grid.columns.begin < grid.columns.count) {
+                const size_t j = next - grid.columns.begin;
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.rows.count; ++i) {
+                    const size_t offset = i * grid.columns.count + j;
+                    const unsigned int candidate = pivotColumn[i] + pivotRow[j];
+                    if (candidate < dist[offset]) {
+                        dist[offset] = candidate;
+                        path[offset] = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
+                    }
                 }
             }
+            const size_t nextSlot = 1 - slot;
+            broadcastPivot(dist, n, next, grid, pivotRows[nextSlot],
+                           pivotColumns[nextSlot], requests[nextSlot]);
+        }
+
+        if (grid.columns.count != 0) {
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.rows.count; ++i) {
+                // Repeating the early row/column updates is idempotent: pivot
+                // distances are unchanged with these nonnegative, bounded weights.
+                updateRow(dist.data() + i * grid.columns.count,
+                          path.data() + i * grid.columns.count, pivotRow,
+                          grid.columns.count, pivotColumn[i],
+                          static_cast<unsigned int>(k));
+            }
         }
     }
 }
 
+std::vector<unsigned int> gatherDistances(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                                         const size_t n, const ProcessGrid& grid,
+                                         const int rank, const MPI_Datatype localType) {
+    std::vector<unsigned int> result;
+    if (rank != 0) {
+        if (!dist.empty()) {
+            MPI_Send(dist.data(), 1, localType, 0, 1, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+        return result;
+    }
+
+    result.resize(n * n);
+    for (int r = 0; r < grid.dimensions[0]; ++r) {
+        const Block rows = blockFor(n, r, grid.dimensions[0]);
+        for (int c = 0; c < grid.dimensions[1]; ++c) {
+            const Block columns = blockFor(n, c, grid.dimensions[1]);
+            if (rows.count == 0 || columns.count == 0) {
+                continue;
+            }
+            const int source = r * grid.dimensions[1] + c;
+            unsigned int* target = result.data() + rows.begin * n + columns.begin;
+            if (source == 0) {
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < rows.count; ++i) {
+                    std::copy_n(dist.data() + i * columns.count, columns.count,
+                                target + i * n);
+                }
+            } else {
+                MPI_Datatype type = matrixBlockType(rows.count, columns.count, n);
+                MPI_Recv(target, 1, type, source, 1, MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                MPI_Type_free(&type);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    return result;
+}

-void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
-                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
+struct Block {
+    size_t begin;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+Block blockFor(const size_t n, const int coordinate, const int parts) {
+    const size_t width = n / parts;
+    const size_t remainder = n % parts;
+    const size_t c = static_cast<size_t>(coordinate);
+    return {c * width + std::min(c, remainder), width + (c < remainder)};
+}
+
+int ownerOf(const size_t k, const size_t n, const int parts) {
+    const size_t width = n / parts;
+    const size_t remainder = n % parts;
+    const size_t boundary = (width + 1) * remainder;
+    // When width is zero, every valid k is in the first branch.
+    return static_cast<int>(k < boundary ? k / (width + 1)
+                                        : remainder + (k - boundary) / width);
+}
+
+struct ProcessGrid {
+    int dimensions[2] = {0, 0};
+    int row;
+    int column;
+    Block rows;
+    Block columns;
+    MPI_Comm rowComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    MPI_Comm columnComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+
+    ProcessGrid(const size_t n, const int rank, const int processes) {
+        MPI_Dims_create(processes, 2, dimensions);
+        row = rank / dimensions[1];
+        column = rank % dimensions[1];
+        rows = blockFor(n, row, dimensions[0]);
+        columns = blockFor(n, column, dimensions[1]);
+        // Communicator ranks coincide with the corresponding grid coordinate.
+        MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, row, column, &rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, column, row, &columnComm);
+    }
+
+    ~ProcessGrid() {
+        MPI_Comm_free(&rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&columnComm);
+    }
+};
+
+MPI_Datatype matrixBlockType(const size_t rows, const size_t columns,
+                            const size_t stride) {
+    MPI_Datatype type;
+    // Using one derived datatype also supports blocks exceeding INT_MAX elements.
+    MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(rows), static_cast<int>(columns),
+                    static_cast<int>(stride), MPI_UNSIGNED, &type);
+    MPI_Type_commit(&type);
+    return type;
+}
+

+                    static_cast<unsigned int>(grid.rows.begin + i));

+        if (!dist.empty()) {
+            MPI_Recv(dist.data(), 1, localType, 0, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD,

+        }

+
+// Separate pivot buffers and non-aliasing rows allow SIMD masked updates.
+inline void updateRow(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                      unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                      const unsigned int* __restrict__ pivotRow,
+                      const size_t columns, const unsigned int distIK,
+                      const unsigned int k) {
+    if (distIK == 0) {
+        return; // The zero-diagonal pivot row cannot improve itself.
+    }
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; ++j) {
+        const unsigned int candidate = distIK + pivotRow[j];
+        if (candidate < dist[j]) {
+            dist[j] = candidate;
+            path[j] = k;
+        }
     }

+void broadcastPivot(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t n,
+                    const size_t k, const ProcessGrid& grid,
+                    std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotRow,
+                    std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotColumn,

+void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t n,
+                   const ProcessGrid& grid) {
+    if (n == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotRows[2] = {
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(grid.columns.count),
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(grid.columns.count)};
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotColumns[2] = {
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(grid.rows.count),
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(grid.rows.count)};
+    MPI_Request requests[2][2];
+    broadcastPivot(dist, n, 0, grid, pivotRows[0], pivotColumns[0], requests[0]);
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
+        const size_t slot = k % 2;
+        MPI_Waitall(2, requests[slot], MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+        const unsigned int* pivotRow = pivotRows[slot].data();
+        const unsigned int* pivotColumn = pivotColumns[slot].data();
+
+        if (k + 1 < n) {
+            // Update the next pivot's row and column first, then communicate them
+            // while the rest of this iteration is computed. All k remain ordered.
+            const size_t next = k + 1;
+            if (next >= grid.rows.begin && next - grid.rows.begin < grid.rows.count
+                && grid.columns.count != 0) {
+                const size_t i = next - grid.rows.begin;
+                updateRow(dist.data() + i * grid.columns.count,
+                          path.data() + i * grid.columns.count, pivotRow,
+                          grid.columns.count, pivotColumn[i],
+                          static_cast<unsigned int>(k));
+            }
+            if (next >= grid.columns.begin &&
+                next - grid.columns.begin < grid.columns.count) {
+                const size_t j = next - grid.columns.begin;
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.rows.count; ++i) {
+                    const size_t offset = i * grid.columns.count + j;
+                    const unsigned int candidate = pivotColumn[i] + pivotRow[j];
+                    if (candidate < dist[offset]) {
+                        dist[offset] = candidate;
+                        path[offset] = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
+                    }
                 }
             }
+            const size_t nextSlot = 1 - slot;
+            broadcastPivot(dist, n, next, grid, pivotRows[nextSlot],
+                           pivotColumns[nextSlot], requests[nextSlot]);
+        }
+
+        if (grid.columns.count != 0) {
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.rows.count; ++i) {
+                // Repeating the early row/column updates is idempotent: pivot
+                // distances are unchanged with these nonnegative, bounded weights.
+                updateRow(dist.data() + i * grid.columns.count,
+                          path.data() + i * grid.columns.count, pivotRow,
+                          grid.columns.count, pivotColumn[i],
+                          static_cast<unsigned int>(k));
+            }
         }
     }
 }
 
+std::vector<unsigned int> gatherDistances(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                                         const size_t n, const ProcessGrid& grid,
+                                         const int rank, const MPI_Datatype localType) {
+    std::vector<unsigned int> result;
+    if (rank != 0) {
+        if (!dist.empty()) {
+            MPI_Send(dist.data(), 1, localType, 0, 1, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+        return result;
+    }
+
+    result.resize(n * n);
+    for (int r = 0; r < grid.dimensions[0]; ++r) {
+        const Block rows = blockFor(n, r, grid.dimensions[0]);
+        for (int c = 0; c < grid.dimensions[1]; ++c) {
+            const Block columns = blockFor(n, c, grid.dimensions[1]);
+            if (rows.count == 0 || columns.count == 0) {
+                continue;
+            }
+            const int source = r * grid.dimensions[1] + c;
+            unsigned int* target = result.data() + rows.begin * n + columns.begin;

-void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
-                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
+struct Block {
+    size_t begin;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+Block blockFor(const size_t n, const int coordinate, const int parts) {
+    const size_t width = n / parts;
+    const size_t remainder = n % parts;
+    const size_t c = static_cast<size_t>(coordinate);
+    return {c * width + std::min(c, remainder), width + (c < remainder)};
+}
+
+int ownerOf(const size_t k, const size_t n, const int parts) {
+    const size_t width = n / parts;
+    const size_t remainder = n % parts;
+    const size_t boundary = (width + 1) * remainder;
+    // When width is zero, every valid k is in the first branch.
+    return static_cast<int>(k < boundary ? k / (width + 1)
+                                        : remainder + (k - boundary) / width);
+}
+
+struct ProcessGrid {
+    int dimensions[2] = {0, 0};
+    int row;
+    int column;
+    Block rows;
+    Block columns;
+    MPI_Comm rowComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    MPI_Comm columnComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+
+    ProcessGrid(const size_t n, const int rank, const int processes) {
+        MPI_Dims_create(processes, 2, dimensions);
+        row = rank / dimensions[1];
+        column = rank % dimensions[1];
+        rows = blockFor(n, row, dimensions[0]);
+        columns = blockFor(n, column, dimensions[1]);
+        // Communicator ranks coincide with the corresponding grid coordinate.
+        MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, row, column, &rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, column, row, &columnComm);
+    }
+
+    ~ProcessGrid() {
+        MPI_Comm_free(&rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&columnComm);
+    }
+};
+
+MPI_Datatype matrixBlockType(const size_t rows, const size_t columns,
+                            const size_t stride) {
+    MPI_Datatype type;
+    // Using one derived datatype also supports blocks exceeding INT_MAX elements.
+    MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(rows), static_cast<int>(columns),
+                    static_cast<int>(stride), MPI_UNSIGNED, &type);
+    MPI_Type_commit(&type);
+    return type;
+}
+

+                    static_cast<unsigned int>(grid.rows.begin + i));
+    }
+
+    if (rank != 0) {
+        if (!dist.empty()) {
+            MPI_Recv(dist.data(), 1, localType, 0, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                     MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+        }
+        return;
+    }

+
+// Separate pivot buffers and non-aliasing rows allow SIMD masked updates.
+inline void updateRow(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                      unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                      const unsigned int* __restrict__ pivotRow,
+                      const size_t columns, const unsigned int distIK,
+                      const unsigned int k) {
+    if (distIK == 0) {
+        return; // The zero-diagonal pivot row cannot improve itself.
+    }
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; ++j) {
+        const unsigned int candidate = distIK + pivotRow[j];
+        if (candidate < dist[j]) {
+            dist[j] = candidate;
+            path[j] = k;
+        }
     }

+void broadcastPivot(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t n,
+                    const size_t k, const ProcessGrid& grid,
+                    std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotRow,
+                    std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotColumn,
+                    MPI_Request* requests) {
+    const int rowOwner = ownerOf(k, n, grid.dimensions[0]);
+    const int columnOwner = ownerOf(k, n, grid.dimensions[1]);
+    if (grid.row == rowOwner && grid.columns.count != 0) {
+        std::copy_n(dist.data() + (k - grid.rows.begin) * grid.columns.count,
+                    grid.columns.count, pivotRow.data());
+    }
+    if (grid.column == columnOwner) {
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.rows.count; ++i) {
+            pivotColumn[i] = dist[i * grid.columns.count + k - grid.columns.begin];
         }

     }
+    MPI_Ibcast(pivotRow.data(), static_cast<int>(grid.columns.count), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+               rowOwner, grid.columnComm, &requests[0]);
+    MPI_Ibcast(pivotColumn.data(), static_cast<int>(grid.rows.count), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+               columnOwner, grid.rowComm, &requests[1]);
 }
 
-void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
-                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,

-    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
-    // For each intermediate node k
-    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
-        // For each source node i
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
-            // For each destination node j
-            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {

-                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];

-

-                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
-                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
+void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t n,
+                   const ProcessGrid& grid) {
+    if (n == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotRows[2] = {
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(grid.columns.count),
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(grid.columns.count)};
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotColumns[2] = {
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(grid.rows.count),
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(grid.rows.count)};
+    MPI_Request requests[2][2];
+    broadcastPivot(dist, n, 0, grid, pivotRows[0], pivotColumns[0], requests[0]);
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
+        const size_t slot = k % 2;
+        MPI_Waitall(2, requests[slot], MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+        const unsigned int* pivotRow = pivotRows[slot].data();
+        const unsigned int* pivotColumn = pivotColumns[slot].data();
+
+        if (k + 1 < n) {
+            // Update the next pivot's row and column first, then communicate them
+            // while the rest of this iteration is computed. All k remain ordered.
+            const size_t next = k + 1;
+            if (next >= grid.rows.begin && next - grid.rows.begin < grid.rows.count
+                && grid.columns.count != 0) {
+                const size_t i = next - grid.rows.begin;
+                updateRow(dist.data() + i * grid.columns.count,
+                          path.data() + i * grid.columns.count, pivotRow,
+                          grid.columns.count, pivotColumn[i],
+                          static_cast<unsigned int>(k));
+            }
+            if (next >= grid.columns.begin &&
+                next - grid.columns.begin < grid.columns.count) {
+                const size_t j = next - grid.columns.begin;
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.rows.count; ++i) {
+                    const size_t offset = i * grid.columns.count + j;
+                    const unsigned int candidate = pivotColumn[i] + pivotRow[j];
+                    if (candidate < dist[offset]) {
+                        dist[offset] = candidate;
+                        path[offset] = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
+                    }
                 }
             }
+            const size_t nextSlot = 1 - slot;
+            broadcastPivot(dist, n, next, grid, pivotRows[nextSlot],
+                           pivotColumns[nextSlot], requests[nextSlot]);
+        }
+
+        if (grid.columns.count != 0) {
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.rows.count; ++i) {
+                // Repeating the early row/column updates is idempotent: pivot
+                // distances are unchanged with these nonnegative, bounded weights.
+                updateRow(dist.data() + i * grid.columns.count,
+                          path.data() + i * grid.columns.count, pivotRow,
+                          grid.columns.count, pivotColumn[i],
+                          static_cast<unsigned int>(k));
+            }
         }
     }
 }
 
+std::vector<unsigned int> gatherDistances(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                                         const size_t n, const ProcessGrid& grid,
+                                         const int rank, const MPI_Datatype localType) {
+    std::vector<unsigned int> result;
+    if (rank != 0) {
+        if (!dist.empty()) {
+            MPI_Send(dist.data(), 1, localType, 0, 1, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+        return result;
+    }
+
+    result.resize(n * n);
+    for (int r = 0; r < grid.dimensions[0]; ++r) {
+        const Block rows = blockFor(n, r, grid.dimensions[0]);
+        for (int c = 0; c < grid.dimensions[1]; ++c) {
+            const Block columns = blockFor(n, c, grid.dimensions[1]);
+            if (rows.count == 0 || columns.count == 0) {
+                continue;
+            }
+            const int source = r * grid.dimensions[1] + c;
+            unsigned int* target = result.data() + rows.begin * n + columns.begin;
+            if (source == 0) {
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < rows.count; ++i) {
+                    std::copy_n(dist.data() + i * columns.count, columns.count,
+                                target + i * n);
+                }
+            } else {
+                MPI_Datatype type = matrixBlockType(rows.count, columns.count, n);
+                MPI_Recv(target, 1, type, source, 1, MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                         MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                MPI_Type_free(&type);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    return result;
+}
+

     // Basic sanity checks

-void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
-                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
+struct Block {
+    size_t begin;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+Block blockFor(const size_t n, const int coordinate, const int parts) {
+    const size_t width = n / parts;
+    const size_t remainder = n % parts;
+    const size_t c = static_cast<size_t>(coordinate);
+    return {c * width + std::min(c, remainder), width + (c < remainder)};
+}
+
+int ownerOf(const size_t k, const size_t n, const int parts) {
+    const size_t width = n / parts;
+    const size_t remainder = n % parts;
+    const size_t boundary = (width + 1) * remainder;
+    // When width is zero, every valid k is in the first branch.
+    return static_cast<int>(k < boundary ? k / (width + 1)
+                                        : remainder + (k - boundary) / width);
+}
+
+struct ProcessGrid {
+    int dimensions[2] = {0, 0};
+    int row;
+    int column;
+    Block rows;
+    Block columns;
+    MPI_Comm rowComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    MPI_Comm columnComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+
+    ProcessGrid(const size_t n, const int rank, const int processes) {
+        MPI_Dims_create(processes, 2, dimensions);
+        row = rank / dimensions[1];
+        column = rank % dimensions[1];
+        rows = blockFor(n, row, dimensions[0]);
+        columns = blockFor(n, column, dimensions[1]);
+        // Communicator ranks coincide with the corresponding grid coordinate.
+        MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, row, column, &rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, column, row, &columnComm);
+    }
+
+    ~ProcessGrid() {
+        MPI_Comm_free(&rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&columnComm);
+    }
+};
+
+MPI_Datatype matrixBlockType(const size_t rows, const size_t columns,
+                            const size_t stride) {
+    MPI_Datatype type;
+    // Using one derived datatype also supports blocks exceeding INT_MAX elements.
+    MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(rows), static_cast<int>(columns),
+                    static_cast<int>(stride), MPI_UNSIGNED, &type);
+    MPI_Type_commit(&type);
+    return type;
+}
+

+        std::fill_n(path.data() + i * grid.columns.count, grid.columns.count,
+                    static_cast<unsigned int>(grid.rows.begin + i));
+    }
+
+    if (rank != 0) {
+        if (!dist.empty()) {
+            MPI_Recv(dist.data(), 1, localType, 0, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                     MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+        }
+        return;

+    const double range = static_cast<double>(MAX_DISTANCE);
+    std::vector<unsigned int> strip;
+    // Generate the original rand_r stream in order, staging only one row strip.
+    // No process retains a replicated global matrix during the computation.
+    for (int r = 0; r < grid.dimensions[0]; ++r) {
+        const Block rows = blockFor(n, r, grid.dimensions[0]);
+        if (rows.count == 0) {
+            continue;
+        }
+        strip.resize(rows.count * n);
+        for (auto& value : strip) {
+            value = 1 + static_cast<unsigned int>(range * rand_r(&seed) /
+                                                  static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX));

+        for (size_t i = 0; i < rows.count; ++i) {

+                    std::copy_n(strip.data() + i * n + columns.begin,
+                                columns.count, dist.data() + i * columns.count);
+                }

+
+// Separate pivot buffers and non-aliasing rows allow SIMD masked updates.
+inline void updateRow(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                      unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                      const unsigned int* __restrict__ pivotRow,
+                      const size_t columns, const unsigned int distIK,
+                      const unsigned int k) {
+    if (distIK == 0) {
+        return; // The zero-diagonal pivot row cannot improve itself.
+    }
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; ++j) {
+        const unsigned int candidate = distIK + pivotRow[j];
+        if (candidate < dist[j]) {
+            dist[j] = candidate;
+            path[j] = k;
+        }
     }

-void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {

+void broadcastPivot(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t n,
+                    const size_t k, const ProcessGrid& grid,
+                    std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotRow,
+                    std::vector<unsigned int>& pivotColumn,
+                    MPI_Request* requests) {
+    const int rowOwner = ownerOf(k, n, grid.dimensions[0]);
+    const int columnOwner = ownerOf(k, n, grid.dimensions[1]);
+    if (grid.row == rowOwner && grid.columns.count != 0) {
+        std::copy_n(dist.data() + (k - grid.rows.begin) * grid.columns.count,
+                    grid.columns.count, pivotRow.data());
+    }
+    if (grid.column == columnOwner) {
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.rows.count; ++i) {
+            pivotColumn[i] = dist[i * grid.columns.count + k - grid.columns.begin];
         }
-        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
     }
+    MPI_Ibcast(pivotRow.data(), static_cast<int>(grid.columns.count), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+               rowOwner, grid.columnComm, &requests[0]);
+    MPI_Ibcast(pivotColumn.data(), static_cast<int>(grid.rows.count), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+               columnOwner, grid.rowComm, &requests[1]);
 }
 
-void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
-                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
-                   const size_t numNodes) {
-    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
-    // For each intermediate node k
-    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
-        // For each source node i
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
-            // For each destination node j
-            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
-                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
-                
-                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
-                
-                if (newDist < distIJ) {
-                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
-                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
+void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t n,
+                   const ProcessGrid& grid) {
+    if (n == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotRows[2] = {
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(grid.columns.count),
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(grid.columns.count)};
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotColumns[2] = {
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(grid.rows.count),
+        std::vector<unsigned int>(grid.rows.count)};
+    MPI_Request requests[2][2];
+    broadcastPivot(dist, n, 0, grid, pivotRows[0], pivotColumns[0], requests[0]);
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
+        const size_t slot = k % 2;
+        MPI_Waitall(2, requests[slot], MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+        const unsigned int* pivotRow = pivotRows[slot].data();
+        const unsigned int* pivotColumn = pivotColumns[slot].data();
+
+        if (k + 1 < n) {
+            // Update the next pivot's row and column first, then communicate them
+            // while the rest of this iteration is computed. All k remain ordered.
+            const size_t next = k + 1;
+            if (next >= grid.rows.begin && next - grid.rows.begin < grid.rows.count
+                && grid.columns.count != 0) {
+                const size_t i = next - grid.rows.begin;
+                updateRow(dist.data() + i * grid.columns.count,
+                          path.data() + i * grid.columns.count, pivotRow,
+                          grid.columns.count, pivotColumn[i],
+                          static_cast<unsigned int>(k));
+            }
+            if (next >= grid.columns.begin &&
+                next - grid.columns.begin < grid.columns.count) {
+                const size_t j = next - grid.columns.begin;
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.rows.count; ++i) {
+                    const size_t offset = i * grid.columns.count + j;
+                    const unsigned int candidate = pivotColumn[i] + pivotRow[j];
+                    if (candidate < dist[offset]) {
+                        dist[offset] = candidate;
+                        path[offset] = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);
+                    }
                 }
             }
+            const size_t nextSlot = 1 - slot;
+            broadcastPivot(dist, n, next, grid, pivotRows[nextSlot],
+                           pivotColumns[nextSlot], requests[nextSlot]);
+        }
+
+        if (grid.columns.count != 0) {
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.rows.count; ++i) {
+                // Repeating the early row/column updates is idempotent: pivot
+                // distances are unchanged with these nonnegative, bounded weights.
+                updateRow(dist.data() + i * grid.columns.count,
+                          path.data() + i * grid.columns.count, pivotRow,
+                          grid.columns.count, pivotColumn[i],
+                          static_cast<unsigned int>(k));
+            }
         }
     }
 }
 
+std::vector<unsigned int> gatherDistances(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                                         const size_t n, const ProcessGrid& grid,
+                                         const int rank, const MPI_Datatype localType) {
+    std::vector<unsigned int> result;
+    if (rank != 0) {
+        if (!dist.empty()) {
+            MPI_Send(dist.data(), 1, localType, 0, 1, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+        return result;
+    }
+
+    result.resize(n * n);
+    for (int r = 0; r < grid.dimensions[0]; ++r) {
+        const Block rows = blockFor(n, r, grid.dimensions[0]);
+        for (int c = 0; c < grid.dimensions[1]; ++c) {
+            const Block columns = blockFor(n, c, grid.dimensions[1]);
+            if (rows.count == 0 || columns.count == 0) {
+                continue;
+            }
+            const int source = r * grid.dimensions[1] + c;
+            unsigned int* target = result.data() + rows.begin * n + columns.begin;
+            if (source == 0) {
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < rows.count; ++i) {
+                    std::copy_n(dist.data() + i * columns.count, columns.count,
+                                target + i * n);
